## `AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Cell QA Specialist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Raw Milk Somatic Cell & Antibiotic Residue Lab
- **Intro:** Cell QA Specialist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Raw Milk Somatic Cell & Antibiotic Residue Lab — Pre-Operational Environmental Verification: Temperature: 20-25°C; Relative Humidity: 30-50% RH; No visible particulate contamination on work surfaces.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 20-25°C; Relative Humidity: 30-50% RH; No visible particulate contamination on work surfaces.
- **Standards:** `ISO 13366-1:2008`, `ISO 13366-2:2006`, `ISO 4833-1:2013`, `Method 995.04`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)

## `AFG-0141-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Cell QA Specialist
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Raw Milk Somatic Cell & Antibiotic Residue Lab
